    gl.clearColor(0.010, 0.024, 0.039, 1);
    gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT | gl.DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);
    gl.enable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);
    gl.depthFunc(gl.LEQUAL);
    gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
    gl.blendFunc(gl.SRC_ALPHA, gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
    gl.disable(gl.CULL_FACE);
    gl.useProgram(program);
    const camera = createCameraAndView(canvas.width / canvas.height);
    gl.uniformMatrix4fv(locations.viewProjection, false, camera.matrix);
    if (locations.eye) gl.uniform3fv(locations.eye, new Float32Array(camera.eye));
    drawBuffer(staticSolidBuffer, staticVertexCount, gl.TRIANGLES);
    const geometry = buildDynamicGeometry(timestamp || performance.now());
    uploadDynamic(dynamicSolidBuffer, geometry.solid);
    uploadDynamic(overlayBuffer, geometry.overlays);
    uploadDynamic(lineBuffer, geometry.lines);
    dynamicVertexCount = geometry.solid.length / VERTEX_STRIDE;
    overlayVertexCount = geometry.overlays.length / VERTEX_STRIDE;
    lineVertexCount = geometry.lines.length / VERTEX_STRIDE;
    drawBuffer(dynamicSolidBuffer, dynamicVertexCount, gl.TRIANGLES);
    gl.depthMask(false);
    drawBuffer(overlayBuffer, overlayVertexCount, gl.TRIANGLES);
    drawBuffer(staticLineBuffer, staticLineCount, gl.LINES);
    drawBuffer(lineBuffer, lineVertexCount, gl.LINES);
    gl.depthMask(true);
    lastRenderMs = performance.now() - started;
    renderSamples.push(lastRenderMs);
    if (renderSamples.length > 180) renderSamples.shift();
    lastFrameMs = lastTimestamp ? timestamp - lastTimestamp : 0;
    if (lastFrameMs > 0 && Number.isFinite(lastFrameMs)) {
      frameSamples.push(lastFrameMs);
      if (frameSamples.length > 180) frameSamples.shift();
    }
    lastTimestamp = timestamp;
    frames += 1;
    if (!lastSecondAt) lastSecondAt = timestamp;
    if (timestamp - lastSecondAt >= 1000) {
      fps = Math.round((frames * 1000) / (timestamp - lastSecondAt));
      frames = 0;
      lastSecondAt = timestamp;
    }
    const sorted = [...frameSamples].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    p95FrameMs = sorted.length ? sorted[Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.floor((sorted.length - 1) * 0.95))] : 0;
    averageRenderMs = renderSamples.reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0) / Math.max(1, renderSamples.length);
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
  }

  function projectTriangles(data, matrix, eye = [6, 13, 18]) {
    const triangles = [];
    const keyLight = normalize([-0.52, 0.86, 0.42]);
    const fillLight = normalize([0.72, 0.22, -0.54]);
    const fogColor = [0.012, 0.026, 0.041];
    for (let i = 0; i + VERTEX_STRIDE * 3 <= data.length; i += VERTEX_STRIDE * 3) {
      const a = transformPoint(matrix, [data[i],data[i+1],data[i+2]]);
      const b = transformPoint(matrix, [data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE],data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+1],data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+2]]);
      const c = transformPoint(matrix, [data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2],data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2+1],data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2+2]]);
      if (![a[0],a[1],a[2],b[0],b[1],b[2],c[0],c[1],c[2]].every(Number.isFinite)) continue;
      if (Math.min(a[0],b[0],c[0]) > 1.6 || Math.max(a[0],b[0],c[0]) < -1.6 || Math.min(a[1],b[1],c[1]) > 1.6 || Math.max(a[1],b[1],c[1]) < -1.6) continue;
      const depth = (a[2]+b[2]+c[2])/3;
      const normal = normalize([data[i+3]+data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+3]+data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2+3],data[i+4]+data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+4]+data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2+4],data[i+5]+data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+5]+data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2+5]]);
      const center = [(data[i] + data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE] + data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2]) / 3, (data[i+1] + data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+1] + data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2+1]) / 3, (data[i+2] + data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+2] + data[i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2+2]) / 3];
      const key = Math.max(0,normal[0]*keyLight[0]+normal[1]*keyLight[1]+normal[2]*keyLight[2]);
      const fill = Math.max(0,normal[0]*fillLight[0]+normal[1]*fillLight[1]+normal[2]*fillLight[2]);
      const illumination = .36 + key*.50 + fill*.14;
      const viewDirection = normalize([eye[0]-center[0],eye[1]-center[1],eye[2]-center[2]]);
      const rim = Math.pow(1 - Math.max(0,normal[0]*viewDirection[0]+normal[1]*viewDirection[1]+normal[2]*viewDirection[2]),2.2);
      const fog = Math.max(0, Math.min(.18, (depth + .05) * .14));
      const offset=i+6, alpha=data[offset+3];
      const lit = [0,1,2].map((channel) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, data[offset+channel]*illumination + [0.025,0.105,0.135][channel]*rim*.55)));
      const graded = lit.map((value,channel) => value*(1-fog)+fogColor[channel]*fog);
      const r=Math.round(graded[0]*255), g=Math.round(graded[1]*255), bl=Math.round(graded[2]*255);
      triangles.push({depth,points:[a,b,c],fill:`rgba(${r},${g},${bl},${alpha})`});
    }
    triangles.sort((a,b)=>b.depth-a.depth);
    return triangles;
  }

  function drawProjectedTriangles(targetCtx, triangles, cssWidth, cssHeight) {
    for (const triangle of triangles) {
      targetCtx.beginPath();
      targetCtx.moveTo((triangle.points[0][0]+1)*.5*cssWidth, (1-triangle.points[0][1])*.5*cssHeight);
      targetCtx.lineTo((triangle.points[1][0]+1)*.5*cssWidth, (1-triangle.points[1][1])*.5*cssHeight);
      targetCtx.lineTo((triangle.points[2][0]+1)*.5*cssWidth, (1-triangle.points[2][1])*.5*cssHeight);
      targetCtx.closePath(); targetCtx.fillStyle=triangle.fill; targetCtx.fill();
    }
  }

  function paintCinematicBackground(targetCtx, cssWidth, cssHeight) {
    const radius = Math.max(cssWidth, cssHeight) * 0.78;
    const atmosphere = targetCtx.createRadialGradient(cssWidth * 0.48, cssHeight * 0.40, 0, cssWidth * 0.48, cssHeight * 0.40, radius);
    atmosphere.addColorStop(0, '#102936');
    atmosphere.addColorStop(0.38, '#091b26');
    atmosphere.addColorStop(0.76, '#06111a');
    atmosphere.addColorStop(1, '#02060b');
    targetCtx.fillStyle = atmosphere;
    targetCtx.fillRect(0, 0, cssWidth, cssHeight);
    const vignette = targetCtx.createRadialGradient(cssWidth * .5, cssHeight * .44, Math.min(cssWidth, cssHeight) * .18, cssWidth * .5, cssHeight * .44, radius);
    vignette.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(1, 5, 10, 0)');
    vignette.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(1, 4, 8, .38)');
    targetCtx.fillStyle = vignette;
    targetCtx.fillRect(0, 0, cssWidth, cssHeight);
  }

  function drawProjectedLines(targetCtx, lines, matrix, cssWidth, cssHeight) {
    for (let i=0; i+VERTEX_STRIDE*2<=lines.length; i+=VERTEX_STRIDE*2) {
      const a=transformPoint(matrix,[lines[i],lines[i+1],lines[i+2]]);
      const b=transformPoint(matrix,[lines[i+VERTEX_STRIDE],lines[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+1],lines[i+VERTEX_STRIDE+2]]);
      const c=lines[i+6],g=lines[i+7],bl=lines[i+8],alpha=lines[i+9];
      targetCtx.strokeStyle=`rgba(${Math.round(c*255)},${Math.round(g*255)},${Math.round(bl*255)},${alpha})`;
      targetCtx.lineWidth=1.5; targetCtx.beginPath(); targetCtx.moveTo((a[0]+1)*.5*cssWidth,(1-a[1])*.5*cssHeight); targetCtx.lineTo((b[0]+1)*.5*cssWidth,(1-b[1])*.5*cssHeight); targetCtx.stroke();
    }
  }

  function renderFallbackFrame(timestamp, started, rect, dpr) {
    const ctx = ctx2d;
    if (!ctx) return;
    const cssWidth = rect.width, cssHeight = rect.height;
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cssWidth, cssHeight);
    const camera = createCameraAndView(canvas.width / canvas.height);
    const geometry = buildDynamicGeometry(timestamp || performance.now());
    // Rasterize the static, lit mesh once per camera/viewport state. Each live frame then composites that buffer and draws only dynamic actors/routes.
    const viewKey = `${yaw.toFixed(3)}|${pitch.toFixed(3)}|${distance.toFixed(2)}|${(rect.width/rect.height).toFixed(4)}|${canvas.width}x${canvas.height}|${dpr}`;
    if (!fallbackStaticCanvas) fallbackStaticCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    const cacheNeedsRebuild = !fallbackStaticTriangles || fallbackStaticKey !== viewKey || fallbackStaticCanvas.width !== canvas.width || fallbackStaticCanvas.height !== canvas.height;
    if (cacheNeedsRebuild) {
      fallbackStaticTriangles = projectTriangles(staticSolid, camera.matrix, camera.eye);
      fallbackStaticKey = viewKey;
      fallbackStaticCacheBuilds += 1;
      fallbackStaticCanvas.width = canvas.width;
      fallbackStaticCanvas.height = canvas.height;
      const staticCtx = fallbackStaticCanvas.getContext('2d');
      if (staticCtx) {
        staticCtx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
        staticCtx.clearRect(0, 0, cssWidth, cssHeight);
        paintCinematicBackground(staticCtx, cssWidth, cssHeight);
        drawProjectedTriangles(staticCtx, fallbackStaticTriangles, cssWidth, cssHeight);
        drawProjectedLines(staticCtx, staticLines, camera.matrix, cssWidth, cssHeight);
      }
    } else { fallbackStaticCacheHits += 1; }
    if (fallbackStaticCanvas) ctx.drawImage(fallbackStaticCanvas, 0, 0, cssWidth, cssHeight);
    else { paintCinematicBackground(ctx, cssWidth, cssHeight); drawProjectedTriangles(ctx, projectTriangles(staticSolid, camera.matrix, camera.eye), cssWidth, cssHeight); }
    const dynamicTriangles = projectTriangles(geometry.solid.concat(geometry.overlays), camera.matrix, camera.eye);
    drawProjectedTriangles(ctx, dynamicTriangles, cssWidth, cssHeight);
    // Tactical route lines are deliberately drawn last, above actors, so their navigation cues remain visible.
    drawProjectedLines(ctx, geometry.lines, camera.matrix, cssWidth, cssHeight);
    lastRenderMs=performance.now()-started; renderSamples.push(lastRenderMs); if(renderSamples.length>180) renderSamples.shift();
    lastFrameMs=lastTimestamp?timestamp-lastTimestamp:0; if(lastFrameMs>0&&Number.isFinite(lastFrameMs)){frameSamples.push(lastFrameMs);if(frameSamples.length>180)frameSamples.shift();}
    lastTimestamp=timestamp; frames++; if(!lastSecondAt)lastSecondAt=timestamp; if(timestamp-lastSecondAt>=1000){fps=Math.round(frames*1000/(timestamp-lastSecondAt));frames=0;lastSecondAt=timestamp;}
    const sorted=[...frameSamples].sort((a,b)=>a-b);p95FrameMs=sorted.length?sorted[Math.min(sorted.length-1,Math.floor((sorted.length-1)*.95))]:0;averageRenderMs=renderSamples.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+v,0)/Math.max(1,renderSamples.length);
    dynamicVertexCount=geometry.solid.length/VERTEX_STRIDE;overlayVertexCount=geometry.overlays.length/VERTEX_STRIDE;lineVertexCount=(geometry.lines.length+staticLines.length)/VERTEX_STRIDE;
    raf=requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
  }

  function setEnabled(next) {
    enabled = Boolean(next) && initialized && !contextLost;
    canvas?.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!enabled));
    if (enabled) {
      resize();
      if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
    } else if (raf) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = 0;
    }
  }

  function resize() {
    if (!canvas || (!gl && !fallback2d)) return;
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cssWidth = Math.max(240, rect.width || canvas.parentElement?.clientWidth || 600);
    const cssHeight = cssWidth * 2 / 3;
    canvas.style.height = `${cssHeight}px`;
    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, qualityCap);
    const width = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssWidth * dpr));
    const height = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssHeight * dpr));
    if (canvas.width !== width || canvas.height !== height) {
      canvas.width = width; canvas.height = height;
      if (gl) gl.viewport(0, 0, width, height);
    }
    if (enabled && !raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
  }

  function setQuality(profile, cap) {
    qualityProfile = ['auto', 'cinematic', 'balanced', 'performance'].includes(profile) ? profile : 'balanced';
    const minimum = qualityProfile === 'auto' ? .75 : 1;
    qualityCap = Math.max(minimum, Math.min(2, Number(cap) || 1.5));
    resize();
  }

  function setScene(scene) {
    lastScene = scene;
    if (enabled && !raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
  }

  function setCallbacks(next) { callbacks = { ...callbacks, ...(next || {}) }; }

  function onPointerDown(event) {
    if (!enabled) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    cameraMotion = null;
    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
    pointers.set(event.pointerId, { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY });
    if (pointers.size === 1) {
      pointerStart = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, yaw, pitch, pointerId: event.pointerId };
      pointerMoved = false;
    } else if (pointers.size === 2) {
      const points = [...pointers.values()];
      lastPinchDistance = Math.hypot(points[0].x-points[1].x, points[0].y-points[1].y);
      pointerMoved = true;
      pointerStart = null;
    }
  }

  function onPointerMove(event) {
    if (!enabled) return;
    if (pointers.has(event.pointerId)) {
      const previous = pointers.get(event.pointerId);
      pointers.set(event.pointerId, { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY });
      if (pointers.size >= 2) {
        const points = [...pointers.values()];
        const nextDistance = Math.hypot(points[0].x-points[1].x, points[0].y-points[1].y);
        if (lastPinchDistance > 0) distance = clamp(distance * (lastPinchDistance / Math.max(1, nextDistance)), 10, 26);
        lastPinchDistance = nextDistance;
        return;
      }
      if (pointerStart) {
        const dx = event.clientX - pointerStart.x;
        const dy = event.clientY - pointerStart.y;
        if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 5) pointerMoved = true;
        if (pointerMoved) {
          yaw = pointerStart.yaw + dx * 0.008;
          pitch = clamp(pointerStart.pitch - dy * 0.006, 0.22, 1.18);
        }
      }
      if (previous && Math.hypot(event.clientX-previous.x, event.clientY-previous.y) > 0) return;
    } else if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') {
      const cell = pickCell(event.clientX, event.clientY);
      const changed = Boolean(cell) !== Boolean(hoveredCell) || (cell && hoveredCell && (cell.x !== hoveredCell.x || cell.y !== hoveredCell.y));
      hoveredCell = cell;
      if (changed && callbacks.onCellHover) callbacks.onCellHover(cell);
    }
  }

  function onPointerUp(event) {
    if (!pointers.has(event.pointerId)) return;
    const wasSingle = pointers.size === 1;
    pointers.delete(event.pointerId);
    try { canvas.releasePointerCapture?.(event.pointerId); } catch (_) { /* pointer already released */ }
    if (wasSingle && !pointerMoved && pointerStart) {
      const cellFirst = Boolean(lastScene && lastScene.decoyTargetMode);
      if (cellFirst) {
        const cell = pickCell(event.clientX, event.clientY);
        if (cell && callbacks.onCellClick) callbacks.onCellClick(cell);
      } else {
        const object = pickObject(event.clientX, event.clientY);
        if (object && callbacks.onObjectClick) callbacks.onObjectClick(object);
        else {
          const cell = pickCell(event.clientX, event.clientY);
          if (cell && callbacks.onCellClick) callbacks.onCellClick(cell);
        }
      }
    }
    if (pointers.size < 2) lastPinchDistance = 0;
    if (pointers.size === 0) pointerStart = null;
    if (pointers.size === 1) {
      const [id, point] = [...pointers.entries()][0];
      pointerStart = { x: point.x, y: point.y, yaw, pitch, pointerId: id };
      pointerMoved = true;
    }
  }

  function onWheel(event) {
    if (!enabled) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    cameraMotion = null;
    distance = clamp(distance + Math.sign(event.deltaY) * 0.7, 10, 26);
  }

  function onLostContext(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    contextLost = true;
    enabled = false;
    if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    raf = 0;
    callbacks.onStatus?.('WebGL потерял графический контекст. Переключись обратно в 2D и обнови страницу.');
  }

  function onRestoredContext() {
    contextLost = false;
    try {
      initProgram();
      buildStaticGeometry();
      initialized = true;
      if (enabled) setEnabled(true);
    } catch (error) {
      callbacks.onStatus?.(`Не удалось восстановить 3D-сцену: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  function clamp(value, min, max) { return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value)); }

  function attach(element) {
    canvas = element;
    if (!canvas) return false;
    try {
      gl = canvas.getContext('webgl', { alpha: false, antialias: true, depth: true, stencil: false, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
      if (gl) initProgram();
      else { ctx2d = canvas.getContext('2d'); fallback2d = Boolean(ctx2d); if (!fallback2d) return false; }
      buildStaticGeometry();
      initialized = true;
      canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, { passive: false });
      canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove, { passive: false });
      canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);
      canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerUp);
      canvas.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });
      canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLostContext, false);
      canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestoredContext, false);
      if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') new ResizeObserver(resize).observe(canvas.parentElement || canvas);
      window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
        if (!document.hidden && enabled && !raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
        if (document.hidden && raf) { cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = 0; }
      });
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      callbacks.onStatus?.(`3D-движок не запустился: ${error.message}`);
      return false;
    }
  }

  function getDiagnostics() {
    const sorted = [...frameSamples].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    const frameMean = frameSamples.length ? frameSamples.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+v,0)/frameSamples.length : null;
    const renderMean = renderSamples.length ? renderSamples.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+v,0)/renderSamples.length : null;
    return {
      supported: Boolean(initialized && (gl || fallback2d) && !contextLost), enabled, renderer: gl ? 'webgl' : fallback2d ? 'canvas2d-software-3d' : 'unavailable',
      fps: frameMean ? Number((1000/frameMean).toFixed(1)) : fps || null,
      frameIntervalAverageMs: frameMean === null ? null : Number(frameMean.toFixed(2)),
      frameIntervalP95Ms: sorted.length ? Number(sorted[Math.min(sorted.length-1, Math.floor((sorted.length-1)*0.95))].toFixed(2)) : null,
      renderAverageMs: renderMean === null ? null : Number(renderMean.toFixed(3)),
      renderLastMs: Number(lastRenderMs.toFixed(3)), drawCalls: enabled ? (gl ? 4 : 1) : 0,
      canvas: canvas ? { width: canvas.width, height: canvas.height } : null,
      staticCache: { builds: fallbackStaticCacheBuilds, reusedFrames: fallbackStaticCacheHits, reusePercent: (fallbackStaticCacheBuilds + fallbackStaticCacheHits) ? Number((fallbackStaticCacheHits / (fallbackStaticCacheBuilds + fallbackStaticCacheHits) * 100).toFixed(1)) : null },
      qualityCap, qualityProfile, camera: { yaw: Number(yaw.toFixed(3)), pitch: Number(pitch.toFixed(3)), distance: Number(distance.toFixed(2)), presetAnimating: Boolean(cameraMotion) }, geometry: { staticVertices: staticVertexCount, dynamicVertices: dynamicVertexCount, overlayVertices: overlayVertexCount, lineVertices: lineVertexCount }, samples: frameSamples.length, vertices: staticVertexCount + dynamicVertexCount + overlayVertexCount + lineVertexCount
    };
  }

  function stop() { setEnabled(false); }

  const api = { attach, setEnabled, setQuality, setScene, setCallbacks, getDiagnostics, pickCell, projectCell, pickObject, projectObject, focusObject, setCameraPreset, zoomBy, resize, stop };
  root.HeistEcho3D = api;
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

</script>

  const GRAPHICS_PROFILES = Object.freeze({
    auto: Object.freeze({ dprCap: 1.5, glowScale: .68, label: 'Авто: подстройка разрешения по измеренным интервалам кадров' }),
    cinematic: Object.freeze({ dprCap: 2, glowScale: 1, label: 'Максимальная чёткость и кинематографичное свечение' }),
    balanced: Object.freeze({ dprCap: 1.5, glowScale: .72, label: 'Фиксированный баланс чёткости и нагрузки' }),
    performance: Object.freeze({ dprCap: 1, glowScale: .42, label: 'Уменьшенная нагрузка на графику для слабых устройств' })
  });
  function effectiveGraphicsCap() {
    return graphicsMode === 'auto' ? autoQualityCap : (GRAPHICS_PROFILES[graphicsMode] || GRAPHICS_PROFILES.cinematic).dprCap;
  }
  function graphicsGlow(value) {
    const profile = GRAPHICS_PROFILES[graphicsMode] || GRAPHICS_PROFILES.cinematic;
    const scale = graphicsMode === 'auto' ? (autoQualityCap < 1.1 ? .42 : autoQualityCap < 1.6 ? .66 : .86) : profile.glowScale;
    return value * scale;
  }
  function currentAutoQualityWindow() {
    const samples = frameIntervalSamples.slice(-90);
    if (samples.length < 45) return null;
    return { average: mean(samples), p95: percentile(samples, .95), slowShare: samples.filter((value) => value > 20).length / samples.length * 100 };
  }
  function updateAdaptiveQuality(timestamp) {
    if (graphicsMode !== 'auto' || timestamp - autoQualityLastDecisionAt < 1800) return false;
    const sample = currentAutoQualityWindow();
    if (!sample || sample.average === null || sample.p95 === null) return false;
    autoQualityLastDecisionAt = timestamp;
    autoQualityDecisionCount += 1;
    const dpr = Math.min(2, Math.max(.75, window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
    let nextCap = autoQualityCap;
    if (sample.p95 > 34 || sample.average > 24 || sample.slowShare > 38) {
      nextCap = Math.max(.75, autoQualityCap - .25);
      autoQualityStableWindows = 0;
      autoQualityReason = `Снижение нагрузки: P95 ${sample.p95.toFixed(1)} мс, медленных кадров ${sample.slowShare.toFixed(0)}%`;
    } else if (sample.p95 < 22 && sample.average < 18 && sample.slowShare < 10) {
      autoQualityStableWindows += 1;
      if (autoQualityStableWindows >= 3) {
        nextCap = Math.min(dpr, autoQualityCap + .25);
        autoQualityStableWindows = 0;
        autoQualityReason = 'Есть запас производительности — качество постепенно повышается';
      }
    } else {
      autoQualityStableWindows = 0;
      autoQualityReason = `Текущий уровень сохраняется: P95 ${sample.p95.toFixed(1)} мс`;
    }
    if (Math.abs(nextCap - autoQualityCap) < .001) return false;
    autoQualityCap = nextCap;
    if (Scene3D && sceneRendererReady) Scene3D.setQuality('auto', autoQualityCap);
    resizeCanvas();
    return true;
  }

  function isReplayReviewing() { return phase === 'finished' && replayHistory.length > 1 && replayCursor < replayHistory.length - 1; }
  function cloneSnapshot(state) { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state)); }

  function recordReplaySnapshot() {
    if (!simState) return;
    const snapshot = cloneSnapshot(simState);
    const last = replayHistory[replayHistory.length - 1];
    if (last && last.tick === snapshot.tick) replayHistory[replayHistory.length - 1] = snapshot;
    else replayHistory.push(snapshot);
    replayCursor = replayHistory.length - 1;
  }

    }
  }

  function blackBoxHash(value){let hash=0x811c9dc5;for(let i=0;i<value.length;i+=1){hash^=value.charCodeAt(i);hash=Math.imul(hash,0x01000193);}return ('00000000'+(hash>>>0).toString(16)).slice(-8);}
  function blackBoxNotice(message,error=false){const toast=document.querySelector('#toast');if(toast){toast.textContent=message;toast.classList.toggle('error',!!error);toast.classList.add('visible');window.setTimeout(()=>toast.classList.remove('visible'),3200);}}
  function compactRunSnapshot(snapshot) {
    return {
      tick:Number(snapshot.tick)||0,status:snapshot.status,seed:Number(snapshot.seed)||0,missionId:snapshot.missionId||snapshot.mission?.id||'operation-glasshouse',
      agents:(snapshot.agents||[]).map(a=>({id:a.id,name:a.name,x:a.x,y:a.y,plan:(a.plan||[]).map(c=>({...c}))})),
      guards:(snapshot.guards||[snapshot.guard].filter(Boolean)).map(g=>({id:g.id,x:g.x,y:g.y,routeIndex:g.routeIndex,mode:g.mode,investigateTarget:g.investigateTarget?{...g.investigateTarget}:null,investigateTicks:g.investigateTicks,suspicion:Number(g.suspicion)||0,lastSeen:g.lastSeen?{...g.lastSeen}:null})),
      guard:snapshot.guard?{x:snapshot.guard.x,y:snapshot.guard.y,routeIndex:snapshot.guard.routeIndex,mode:snapshot.guard.mode,investigateTarget:snapshot.guard.investigateTarget?{...snapshot.guard.investigateTarget}:null,investigateTicks:snapshot.guard.investigateTicks}:null,
      camera:snapshot.camera?{...snapshot.camera}:null,objective:snapshot.objective?{...snapshot.objective}:null,exit:snapshot.exit?{...snapshot.exit}:null,
      gadgets:snapshot.gadgets?{...snapshot.gadgets}:null,events:(snapshot.events||[]).map(e=>({tick:e.tick,kind:e.kind,message:e.message})),result:snapshot.result?{...snapshot.result}:null,
      geometry:snapshot.__architect?{terrainWalls:(snapshot.__architect.terrainWalls||[]).map(p=>[p[0],p[1]]),blockedCommands:Number(snapshot.__architect.blockedCommands)||0,guardDetours:Number(snapshot.__architect.guardDetours)||0}:null
    };
  }
  function buildBlackBoxArchive() {
    if(!simState||phase!=='finished'||replayHistory.length<2)return null;
    const context=window.HEISTECHO_BLACKBOX_CONTEXT?window.HEISTECHO_BLACKBOX_CONTEXT():{};
    const contract=context.contract||{};
    const contractData={id:contract.id,name:contract.name,custom:!!contract.custom,maxTicks:contract.maxTicks,noDecoy:!!contract.noDecoy,noHack:!!contract.noHack,noWarnings:!!contract.noWarnings,guardSpeed:contract.guardSpeed||1,maxCommands:contract.maxCommands??null,terrainWalls:(contract.terrainWalls||[]).map(p=>[p[0],p[1]])};
    const snapshots=replayHistory.map(compactRunSnapshot),plans=Game.clonePlans(simState),release=document.querySelector('[data-heist-echo-release]')?.getAttribute('data-heist-echo-release')||'unknown';
    const mission=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(simState.mission||Game.MISSION));
    const terrainWalls=(contractData.terrainWalls||[]).map(p=>({x:Number(p[0]),y:Number(p[1])})).filter(p=>Number.isInteger(p.x)&&Number.isInteger(p.y));
    mission.walls=[...(mission.walls||[]),...terrainWalls].filter((p,i,a)=>a.findIndex(q=>q.x===p.x&&q.y===p.y)===i);
    const seed=Number(simState.seed)||0;
    const engineTimeline=(simState.timeline||[]).map(frame=>Game.snapshotFrame?Game.snapshotFrame(frame):frame).slice(0,41);
    const stable={release,contract:contractData,plans,snapshots,final:snapshots[snapshots.length-1],mission,seed,engineTimeline};
    const fingerprint=blackBoxHash(JSON.stringify(stable));
    const archive={schema:1,kind:'HEIST-ECHO-BLACKBOX',engineSchema:2,release,exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),contract:contractData,plans,snapshots,report:context.report?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(context.report)):null,mission,seed,engineTimeline,fingerprint};
    archive.integrity=blackBoxHash(JSON.stringify(archive));
    return archive;
  }
  function validateBlackBoxArchive(value) {
    if(!value||typeof value!=='object'||Array.isArray(value)||value.schema!==1||value.kind!=='HEIST-ECHO-BLACKBOX'||!Array.isArray(value.snapshots)||value.snapshots.length<2||value.snapshots.length>41||!value.contract||!value.plans||typeof value.plans!=='object'||typeof value.integrity!=='string')return {ok:false,error:'Формат Black Box не поддерживается.'};
    const copy={...value};delete copy.integrity;
    if(blackBoxHash(JSON.stringify(copy))!==value.integrity)return {ok:false,error:'Контрольная сумма Black Box не совпадает. Архив повреждён или изменён.'};
    if(value.snapshots.some((s,i)=>!s||!Number.isInteger(s.tick)||s.tick<0||s.tick>40||(i&&s.tick<value.snapshots[i-1].tick)))return {ok:false,error:'Последовательность тактов архива повреждена.'};
    if(value.contract.terrainWalls&&(!Array.isArray(value.contract.terrainWalls)||value.contract.terrainWalls.length>10))return {ok:false,error:'Некорректная геометрия в архиве.'};
    if(JSON.stringify(value).length>2500000)return {ok:false,error:'Black Box превышает допустимый размер 2,5 МБ.'};
    const commands=['move','hack','secure','wait','decoy'];
    for(const agentId of ['mira','kite']){
      if(!Array.isArray(value.plans[agentId])||value.plans[agentId].length>40)return {ok:false,error:'Некорректная очередь команд в архиве.'};
      if(value.plans[agentId].some(c=>!c||!commands.includes(c.type)||(c.type==='move'||c.type==='decoy')&&(!Number.isInteger(c.x)||!Number.isInteger(c.y)||c.x<0||c.x>11||c.y<0||c.y>7)))return {ok:false,error:'Архив содержит недопустимую команду.'};
    }
    if(value.mission&&window.HeistEcho?.validateMission){const checked=window.HeistEcho.validateMission(value.mission);if(!checked.ok)return {ok:false,error:`Уровень архива не прошёл проверку: ${checked.error}`};}
    if(value.engineTimeline&&(!Array.isArray(value.engineTimeline)||value.engineTimeline.length<2||value.engineTimeline.length>41||value.engineTimeline.some((f,i)=>!f||!Number.isInteger(f.tick)||f.tick<0||f.tick>40||(i&&f.tick<value.engineTimeline[i-1].tick))))return {ok:false,error:'Повтор операции содержит недопустимую шкалу тактов.'};
    if(value.engineSchema===2&&(!Number.isInteger(value.seed)||value.seed<0||value.seed>4294967295||!Array.isArray(value.engineTimeline)||!value.mission))return {ok:false,error:'В детерминированном архиве не хватает seed, карты или повторной шкалы.'};
    const stable=value.engineSchema===2?{release:value.release,contract:value.contract,plans:value.plans,snapshots:value.snapshots,final:value.snapshots[value.snapshots.length-1],mission:value.mission,seed:value.seed,engineTimeline:value.engineTimeline}:{release:value.release,contract:value.contract,plans:value.plans,snapshots:value.snapshots,final:value.snapshots[value.snapshots.length-1]};
    if(value.fingerprint&&blackBoxHash(JSON.stringify(stable))!==value.fingerprint)return {ok:false,error:'Отпечаток исходных данных не совпадает.'};
    return {ok:true,release:value.release,ticks:value.snapshots[value.snapshots.length-1].tick,fingerprint:value.fingerprint,snapshots:value.snapshots.length,deterministic:value.engineSchema===2};
  }
  function downloadBlackBoxArchive() {
    const archive=buildBlackBoxArchive();
    if(!archive){blackBoxNotice('Black Box станет доступен после завершения операции.',true);return false;}
    const validation=validateBlackBoxArchive(archive);
    if(!validation.ok){blackBoxNotice(validation.error,true);return false;}
    const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(archive,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),anchor=document.createElement('a');
    anchor.href=url;anchor.download=`HEIST-ECHO-BLACKBOX-${String(archive.contract.id).replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/gi,'-')}-T${validation.ticks}.json`;anchor.hidden=true;document.body.append(anchor);anchor.click();anchor.remove();window.setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1200);
    blackBoxNotice(`Black Box экспортирован · ${validation.ticks} тактов · hash ${validation.fingerprint}.`);return true;
  }

  window.HEISTECHO_BLACKBOX_BRIDGE = Object.freeze({build:buildBlackBoxArchive,validate:validateBlackBoxArchive,download:downloadBlackBoxArchive});

  function replayEventLabel(event) {
    const message = String(event && event.message || '');
    if (/disabled the camera/i.test(message)) return 'Камера отключена';
    if (/secured the data case/i.test(message)) return 'Носитель получен';
    if (/operation complete/i.test(message)) return 'Эвакуация завершена';
    if (/entered the active camera zone/i.test(message)) return 'Обнаружение камерой';
    if (/came within one tile/i.test(message)) return 'Обнаружение патрулём';
    if (/operation failed/i.test(message) || event.kind === 'danger') return 'Операция сорвана';
    if (/move blocked/i.test(message)) return 'Маршрут заблокирован';
    if (/out of reach/i.test(message)) return 'Цель вне досягаемости';
    return message.length > 48 ? `${message.slice(0, 45)}…` : message;
  }

  function renderReplayMilestones() {
    if (!ui.replayMilestones) return;
    const significant = (simState && simState.events || []).filter((event) => ['success', 'danger', 'warning'].includes(event.kind));
    const byTick = new Map();
    const rank = { warning: 1, success: 2, danger: 3 };
    for (const event of significant) {
      const previous = byTick.get(event.tick);
      if (!previous || (rank[event.kind] || 0) >= (rank[previous.kind] || 0)) byTick.set(event.tick, event);
    }
    const markers = [...byTick.values()].sort((a, b) => a.tick - b.tick).slice(0, 8);
    const nodes = markers.map((event) => {
      const button = document.createElement('button');
      button.type = 'button';
      button.className = `replay-milestone ${event.kind}${replayHistory[replayCursor] && replayHistory[replayCursor].tick === event.tick ? ' current' : ''}`;
      button.dataset.replayTick = String(event.tick);
      button.setAttribute('aria-current', String(Boolean(replayHistory[replayCursor] && replayHistory[replayCursor].tick === event.tick)));
      button.title = `Перейти к T+${String(event.tick).padStart(2, '0')}: ${event.message}`;
      const tick = document.createElement('small'); tick.textContent = `T+${String(event.tick).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      const label = document.createElement('span'); label.textContent = replayEventLabel(event);
      button.append(tick, label);
      return button;
    });
    ui.replayMilestones.replaceChildren(...nodes);
    ui.replayMilestones.hidden = markers.length === 0;
  }

  function renderReplayControls() {
    if (!ui.replayPanel) return;
    const available = phase === 'finished' && replayHistory.length > 1;
    ui.replayPanel.hidden = !available;
    if (!available) {
      stopReplayPlayback();
      return;
    }
    const selected = replayHistory[Math.max(0, Math.min(replayCursor, replayHistory.length - 1))];
    const finalTick = replayHistory[replayHistory.length - 1].tick;
    ui.replayRange.min = '0';    ui.replayRange.max = String(replayHistory.length - 1);
    ui.replayRange.value = String(replayCursor);
    ui.replayRange.setAttribute('aria-valuetext', `Такт ${selected.tick} из ${finalTick}`);
    ui.replayCurrentTick.textContent = `T+${String(selected.tick).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    ui.replayFinalTick.textContent = `T+${String(finalTick).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    ui.replayStateLabel.textContent = `T+${String(selected.tick).padStart(2, '0')} / ${String(finalTick).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    ui.replayPrev.disabled = replayCursor <= 0 || replayIsPlaying;
    ui.replayNext.disabled = replayCursor >= replayHistory.length - 1 || replayIsPlaying;
    ui.replayPlay.disabled = replayHistory.length < 2;
    ui.replayPlay.textContent = replayIsPlaying ? 'Ⅱ Пауза разбора' : '▶ Воспроизвести разбор';
    ui.replayPlay.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(replayIsPlaying));
    ui.replayHome.disabled = replayCursor >= replayHistory.length - 1;
    if(ui.replayExport)ui.replayExport.disabled=phase!=='finished'||replayHistory.length<2;
    const event = [...selected.events].reverse().find((entry) => entry.tick === selected.tick);
    ui.replayEventDetail.textContent = event ? `T+${String(event.tick).padStart(2, '0')} · ${replayEventLabel(event)}` : `T+${String(selected.tick).padStart(2, '0')} · На этом такте новых записей в журнале нет.`;
    renderReplayMilestones();
  }

  function replayStepForward() {
    if (phase !== 'finished' || replayHistory.length < 2) { stopReplayPlayback(); return; }
    if (replayCursor >= replayHistory.length - 1) {
      stopReplayPlayback();
      renderAll();
      return;
    }
    replayCursor += 1;
    if (replayCursor >= replayHistory.length - 1) stopReplayPlayback();
    renderAll();
  }

  function toggleReplayPlayback() {
    if (phase !== 'finished' || replayHistory.length < 2) return;
    if (replayIsPlaying) {
      stopReplayPlayback();
      renderReplayControls();
      return;
    }
    if (replayCursor >= replayHistory.length - 1) replayCursor = 0;
    replayIsPlaying = true;
    ui.replayPlay.textContent = 'Ⅱ Пауза разбора';
    ui.replayPlay.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    ui.replayPrev.disabled = true;
    ui.replayNext.disabled = true;
    const rate = Math.max(.25, Number(ui.replaySpeed.value) || 1);
    replayTimer = window.setInterval(replayStepForward, 800 / rate);
    renderAll();
  }

  function jumpReplayToTick(tick) {
    if (phase !== 'finished' || replayHistory.length < 2) return;
    stopReplayPlayback();
    const index = replayHistory.findIndex((snapshot) => snapshot.tick === tick);
    if (index >= 0) replayCursor = index;
    renderAll();
  }

  function moveReplayCursor(delta) {
    if (phase !== 'finished' || replayHistory.length < 2) return;
    stopReplayPlayback();
    replayCursor = Math.max(0, Math.min(replayHistory.length - 1, replayCursor + delta));
    renderAll();
  }
  function selectedAgent(state = planState) { return Game.getAgent(state, selectedAgentId); }
  function activeCommandsAllowed() { return phase === 'planning' && planState.status === 'planning'; }
  function showToast(message, isError = false) {
    ui.toast.textContent = message;
    ui.toast.classList.toggle('error', isError);
    ui.toast.classList.add('visible');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer);
    toastTimer = setTimeout(() => ui.toast.classList.remove('visible'), 2450);
  }
  function stopTimer() { if (timer !== null) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; } }
  function setPhase(next) {
    phase = next;
    if (next === 'planning') {
      ui.phase.textContent = 'ПЛАНИРОВАНИЕ';
      ui.execState.textContent = 'ГОТОВ К ЗАПУСКУ';
      ui.execState.className = 'execution-state';
      ui.status.textContent = 'Прокладывай маршруты и добавляй нужные действия. Потом запусти операцию.';
    } else if (next === 'running') {
      ui.phase.textContent = 'СИМУЛЯЦИЯ';
      ui.execState.textContent = isPaused ? 'ПАУЗА' : 'ОПЕРАЦИЯ ИДЁТ';
      ui.execState.className = 'execution-state';
      ui.status.textContent = isPaused ? 'Симуляция приостановлена. Продолжи или пройди следующий такт вручную.' : 'Приказы выполняются. Наблюдай за охраной и состоянием задачи.';
    } else {
      const won = simState && simState.status === 'won';
      ui.phase.textContent = won ? 'УСПЕХ' : 'ПРОВАЛ';
      ui.execState.textContent = won ? 'ОПЕРАЦИЯ ЗАВЕРШЕНА' : 'ТРЕБУЕТСЯ ПЕРЕПЛАНИРОВКА';
      ui.execState.className = `execution-state ${won ? 'success' : 'danger'}`;
      ui.status.textContent = won ? 'Данные извлечены. Можно изучить журнал или изменить план и проверить другой подход.' : (simState && simState.result ? simState.result.reason : 'Операция остановлена.');
    }
    renderControls();
  }

  function pushSample(samples, value, limit = SAMPLE_LIMIT) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) return;
    samples.push(value);
    if (samples.length > limit) samples.splice(0, samples.length - limit);
  }

  function mean(samples) {
    return samples.length ? samples.reduce((total, value) => total + value, 0) / samples.length : null;
  }

  function percentile(samples, fraction) {
    if (!samples.length) return null;
    const sorted = [...samples].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    return sorted[Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.floor((sorted.length - 1) * fraction))];
  }

  function snapshotPositions(state) {
    return {
      guard: { x: state.guard.x, y: state.guard.y },
      agents: Object.fromEntries(state.agents.map((agent) => [agent.id, { x: agent.x, y: agent.y }]))
    };
  }

  function setMotionBetween(before, after) {
    motionFrame = null;
    actionPulse = null;
    if (!cinematicMotionEnabled) return;
    const moved = before.guard.x !== after.guard.x || before.guard.y !== after.guard.y ||
      Object.keys(after.agents).some((id) => before.agents[id] && (before.agents[id].x !== after.agents[id].x || before.agents[id].y !== after.agents[id].y));
    const previousState = simState;
    const rate = Number(ui.speedSelect.value) || 1;
    if (moved) {
      motionFrame = { from: before, to: after, startedAt: performance.now(), duration: Math.min(390, Math.max(150, (BASE_TICK_MS / rate) * .62)) };
    }
    let pulsePoint = null;
    let pulseColor = COLORS.cyan;
    if (before.cameraActive !== after.cameraActive) {
      pulsePoint = { x: Game.MISSION.camera.x, y: Game.MISSION.camera.y };
      pulseColor = COLORS.amber;
    } else if (before.dataSecured !== after.dataSecured) {
      const carrier = previousState && previousState.agents.find((agent) => agent.id === after.carrierId);
      pulsePoint = carrier ? { x: carrier.x, y: carrier.y } : { x: Game.MISSION.objective.x, y: Game.MISSION.objective.y };
      pulseColor = COLORS.green;
    } else {
      const movedAgentId = Object.keys(after.agents).find((id) => before.agents[id] && (before.agents[id].x !== after.agents[id].x || before.agents[id].y !== after.agents[id].y));
      const movedAgent = movedAgentId && simState && simState.agents.find((agent) => agent.id === movedAgentId);
      if (movedAgent) pulsePoint = { x: movedAgent.x, y: movedAgent.y };
      else if (before.guard.x !== after.guard.x || before.guard.y !== after.guard.y) { pulsePoint = { x: after.guard.x, y: after.guard.y }; pulseColor = COLORS.red; }
    }
    if (pulsePoint) actionPulse = { ...pulsePoint, color: pulseColor, startedAt: performance.now(), duration: 560 };
  }

  function visualState() {
    const state = currentState();
    if (!cinematicMotionEnabled || !motionFrame) return state;
    const raw = (performance.now() - motionFrame.startedAt) / motionFrame.duration;
    if (raw >= 1) { motionFrame = null; return state; }
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, raw));
    const eased = t < .5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 3) / 2;
    const blend = (start, finish) => start + (finish - start) * eased;
    const agents = state.agents.map((agent) => {
      const from = motionFrame.from.agents[agent.id];
      const to = motionFrame.to.agents[agent.id];
      return from && to ? { ...agent, x: blend(from.x, to.x), y: blend(from.y, to.y) } : agent;
    });
    return { ...state, guard: { ...state.guard, x: blend(motionFrame.from.guard.x, motionFrame.to.guard.x), y: blend(motionFrame.from.guard.y, motionFrame.to.guard.y) }, agents };
  }

  function syncScene3D() {
    if (!Scene3D || !sceneRendererReady) return;
    Scene3D.setScene({
      state: visualState(), planState, phase, selectedAgentId,
      showCameraLayer, showPatrolLayer,
      hoverCell: keyboardNavigationActive ? null : hoverCell, decoyTargetMode, selectedSceneObjectId,
      keyboardCursor: { ...keyboardCursor }, keyboardNavigationActive,
      mission: Game.MISSION, graphicsMode, cinematicMotionEnabled
    });
  }

  function setBoardView(nextView) {
    if (nextView !== '2d' && nextView !== '3d') return;
    if (nextView === '3d' && !sceneRendererReady) {
      showToast('3D-сцена недоступна в этом браузере. Используй 2D-карту.', true);
      return;
    }
    boardView = nextView;
    if (ui.scene3dControls) ui.scene3dControls.hidden = boardView !== '3d';
    canvas.hidden = boardView === '3d';
    scene3dCanvas.hidden = boardView !== '3d';
    canvasFrame.dataset.view = boardView;
    ui.sceneModeToggle.classList.toggle('active', boardView === '3d');
    ui.sceneModeToggle.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(boardView === '3d'));
    ui.sceneModeToggle.textContent = boardView === '3d' ? '2D КАРТА ↗' : '3D СЦЕНА ↗';
    ui.sceneModeToggle.title = boardView === '3d' ? 'Вернуться к плоской тактической карте' : 'Переключить на интерактивную 3D-сцену';
    Scene3D && Scene3D.setEnabled(boardView === '3d');
    if (boardView === '3d') {
      keyboardNavigationActive = false;
      hoverCell = null;
      syncScene3D();
    }
    renderSceneObjectInspector();
    renderScene3DStatusBar();
    syncScene3D();
    resizeCanvas();
    showToast(boardView === '3d' ? '3D-сцена включена. Перетаскивай для поворота, колесо или щипок — для масштаба.' : 'Плоская карта включена. Правила симуляции не изменились.');
  }

  function updateDraftStatus(message, state = 'saved') {
    if (!ui.draftStatus) return;
    ui.draftStatus.dataset.state = state;
    const label = ui.draftStatus.querySelector('span:last-child');
    if (label) label.textContent = message;
  }

  function persistPlanDraft() {
    try {
      const storage = window.localStorage;
      if (!storage) { updateDraftStatus('ЛОКАЛЬНОЕ ХРАНЕНИЕ НЕДОСТУПНО', 'unavailable'); return false; }
      const payload = {
        schemaVersion: 1,
        missionId: Game.MISSION.id,
        savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        plans: Game.clonePlans(planState)
      };
      storage.setItem(DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
      updateDraftStatus('ЧЕРНОВИК СОХРАНЁН ЛОКАЛЬНО', 'saved');
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      updateDraftStatus('БРАУЗЕР ЗАПРЕТИЛ СОХРАНЕНИЕ', 'unavailable');
      return false;
    }
  }

  function restorePlanDraft() {
    try {
      const storage = window.localStorage;
      if (!storage) { updateDraftStatus('ЛОКАЛЬНОЕ ХРАНЕНИЕ НЕДОСТУПНО', 'unavailable'); return false; }
      const raw = storage.getItem(DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (!raw) { updateDraftStatus('ЧЕРНОВИК БУДЕТ СОХРАНЯТЬСЯ В ЭТОМ БРАУЗЕРЕ', 'ready'); return false; }
      const payload = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!payload || payload.schemaVersion !== 1 || payload.missionId !== Game.MISSION.id || !payload.plans) {
        updateDraftStatus('СТАРЫЙ ЧЕРНОВИК ПРОПУЩЕН', 'unavailable');
        return false;
      }
      const restored = Game.createInitialState();
      for (const agentId of ['mira', 'kite']) {
        const source = Array.isArray(payload.plans[agentId]) ? payload.plans[agentId].slice(0, 80) : [];
        const agent = Game.getAgent(restored, agentId);
        for (const item of source) {
          if (!item || typeof item.type !== 'string') continue;
          if (item.type === Game.ACTION.MOVE) {
            if (!Number.isInteger(item.x) || !Number.isInteger(item.y)) continue;
            const position = Game.getPlannedPosition(restored, agentId);
            if (!position || Game.manhattan(position, item) !== 1 || !Game.isWalkable(restored, item.x, item.y)) continue;
            agent.plan.push({ type: Game.ACTION.MOVE, x: item.x, y: item.y });
          } else if ([Game.ACTION.HACK, Game.ACTION.SECURE, Game.ACTION.WAIT].includes(item.type)) {
            Game.enqueueAction(restored, agentId, item.type);
          } else if (item.type === Game.ACTION.DECOY && Number.isInteger(item.x) && Number.isInteger(item.y)) {
            Game.enqueueAction(restored, agentId, item.type, { target: { x: item.x, y: item.y } });
          }
        }
      }
      planState = restored;
      const restoredCount = restored.agents.reduce((count, agent) => count + agent.plan.length, 0);
      if (restoredCount) {
        keyboardCursor = { ...Game.getPlannedPosition(restored, selectedAgentId) };
        updateDraftStatus(`ЧЕРНОВИК ВОССТАНОВЛЕН · ${String(restoredCount).padStart(2, '0')} ШАГОВ`, 'saved');
        return true;
      }
      updateDraftStatus('ЧЕРНОВИК ПУСТ · ГОТОВ К ПЛАНИРОВАНИЮ', 'ready');
      return false;
    } catch (error) {
      updateDraftStatus('ЛОКАЛЬНОЕ СОХРАНЕНИЕ НЕДОСТУПНО', 'unavailable');
      return false;
    }
  }

  function createAudioContext() {
    if (audioContext) return audioContext;
    const Constructor = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!Constructor) return null;
    try { audioContext = new Constructor(); return audioContext; } catch (error) { return null; }
  }

  function playSound(kind = 'tap') {
    if (!soundEnabled) return;
    const context = createAudioContext();
    if (!context) return;
    if (context.state === 'suspended' && typeof context.resume === 'function') context.resume().catch(() => {});
    const patterns = {
      tap: [[520, 0, .055, .018]],
      hack: [[420, 0, .09, .022], [760, .075, .11, .02]],
      success: [[523, 0, .11, .023], [659, .09, .12, .022], [784, .18, .17, .02]],
      error: [[210, 0, .13, .022], [165, .09, .17, .018]],
      secure: [[660, 0, .09, .021], [990, .08, .14, .019]],
      decoy: [[740, 0, .055, .017], [510, .055, .11, .021], [330, .17, .13, .016]]
    };
    const now = context.currentTime || 0;
    for (const [frequency, offset, duration, volume] of (patterns[kind] || patterns.tap)) {
      try {
        const oscillator = context.createOscillator();
        const gain = context.createGain();
        oscillator.type = kind === 'error' ? 'triangle' : 'sine';
        oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now + offset);
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001, now + offset);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(volume, now + offset + .012);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001, now + offset + duration);
        oscillator.connect(gain); gain.connect(context.destination);
        oscillator.start(now + offset); oscillator.stop(now + offset + duration + .02);
      } catch (error) { /* Audio is an optional enhancement; gameplay continues without it. */ }
    }
  }

  function renderHackPuzzle() {
    if (!ui.hackGrid) return;
    const pathSet = new Set(hackPath);
    ui.hackGrid.querySelectorAll('[data-hack-node]').forEach((button) => {
      const node = Number(button.dataset.hackNode);
      button.classList.toggle('is-path', pathSet.has(node));
      button.classList.toggle('is-current', hackPath[hackPath.length - 1] === node);
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(pathSet.has(node)));
    });
    ui.hackPathStatus.textContent = hackPath.map((node) => String(node).padStart(2, '0')).join('  →  ');
    ui.hackMistakes.textContent = `ОШИБКИ ${String(hackMistakeCount).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }

  function openHackConsole() {
    if (!activeCommandsAllowed()) return;
    const position = Game.getPlannedPosition(planState, selectedAgentId);
    if (!planState.camera.active) { showToast('Камера уже отключена.', true); return; }
    if (planState.agents.some((agent) => agent.plan.some((command) => command.type === Game.ACTION.HACK))) { showToast('Отключение камеры уже есть в плане.', true); return; }
    if (!position || Game.manhattan(position, planState.camera) !== 1) { showToast('Сначала подойди к камере на соседнюю клетку.', true); return; }
    hackPath = [1];
    hackMistakeCount = 0;
    lastHackFocus = document.activeElement;
    ui.hackConsole.hidden = false;
    document.body.classList.add('hack-console-open');
    ui.hackStatus.textContent = 'КАНАЛ ОЖИДАЕТ МАРШРУТ';
    Playtest.recordInteraction(sessionMetrics, 'hackPuzzleOpened', 1, false);
    renderHackPuzzle();
    const firstOpenNode = ui.hackGrid.querySelector('[data-hack-node="4"]');
    if (firstOpenNode && typeof firstOpenNode.focus === 'function') firstOpenNode.focus();
  }

  function closeHackConsole(restoreFocus = true) {
    if (!ui.hackConsole || ui.hackConsole.hidden) return;
    ui.hackConsole.hidden = true;
    document.body.classList.remove('hack-console-open');
    if (restoreFocus && lastHackFocus && typeof lastHackFocus.focus === 'function') lastHackFocus.focus();
    lastHackFocus = null;
  }

  function registerHackMistake(message) {
    hackMistakeCount += 1;
    ui.hackStatus.textContent = message;
    ui.hackStatus.dataset.state = 'error';
    ui.hackConsole.classList.remove('hack-error-flash');
    // Restarting the CSS animation gives tactile visual feedback without changing simulation time.
    void ui.hackConsole.offsetWidth;
    ui.hackConsole.classList.add('hack-error-flash');
    Playtest.recordInteraction(sessionMetrics, 'hackPuzzleMissteps', 1, false);
    playSound('error');
    renderHackPuzzle();
  }

  function onHackNodeClick(node) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(node) || node < 1 || node > 9 || ui.hackConsole.hidden) return;
    if (HACK_BLOCKED_NODES.has(node)) { registerHackMistake(`УЗЕЛ ${String(node).padStart(2, '0')} ПОВРЕЖДЁН · ВЫБЕРИ ДРУГОЙ ПУТЬ`); return; }
    if (node === 1) {
      hackPath = [1];
      ui.hackStatus.textContent = 'СИГНАЛ СБРОШЕН КО ВХОДУ';
      ui.hackStatus.dataset.state = 'ready';
      renderHackPuzzle(); playSound('tap'); return;
    }
    const existingIndex = hackPath.indexOf(node);
    if (existingIndex >= 0 && existingIndex < hackPath.length - 1) {
      hackPath = hackPath.slice(0, existingIndex + 1);
      ui.hackStatus.textContent = 'МАРШРУТ ВОССТАНОВЛЕН';
      ui.hackStatus.dataset.state = 'ready';
      renderHackPuzzle(); playSound('tap'); return;
    }
    const last = hackPath[hackPath.length - 1];
    const ax = (last - 1) % 3, ay = Math.floor((last - 1) / 3);
    const bx = (node - 1) % 3, by = Math.floor((node - 1) / 3);
    if (Math.abs(ax - bx) + Math.abs(ay - by) !== 1) { registerHackMistake('СИГНАЛ ПОТЕРЯН · НУЖЕН СОСЕДНИЙ УЗЕЛ'); return; }
    hackPath.push(node);
    ui.hackStatus.textContent = `СОЕДИНЕНИЕ ${hackPath.length - 1}/4 · СИГНАЛ СТАБИЛЕН`;
    ui.hackStatus.dataset.state = 'ready';
    renderHackPuzzle();
    playSound('tap');
    if (node === 9) {
      ui.hackStatus.textContent = 'КАНАЛ УСТАНОВЛЕН · КАМЕРА ДОСТУПНА';
      Playtest.recordInteraction(sessionMetrics, 'hackPuzzleSolved', 1, false);
      playSound('hack');
      const focusReturn = lastHackFocus;
      closeHackConsole(false);
      addAction(Game.ACTION.HACK);
      if (focusReturn && typeof focusReturn.focus === 'function') focusReturn.focus();
    }
  }

  function startLongTaskObserver() {
    const Observer = window.PerformanceObserver;
    if (!Observer) return;
    try {
      longTaskObserver = new Observer((list) => {
        for (const entry of list.getEntries()) pushSample(longTaskSamples, entry.duration, 120);
      });
      longTaskObserver.observe({ entryTypes: ['longtask'] });
      longTaskObserverSupported = true;
    } catch (error) {
      longTaskObserver = null;
      longTaskObserverSupported = false;
    }
  }

  function diagnosticsSnapshot() {
    const frameMean = mean(frameIntervalSamples);
    const slow = frameIntervalSamples.length ? (frameIntervalSamples.filter((value) => value > 20).length / frameIntervalSamples.length) * 100 : null;
    return {
      measurement: 'runtime browser measurements; no synthetic benchmark values',
      sampledFrameIntervals: frameIntervalSamples.length,
      frameIntervalP95Ms: percentile(frameIntervalSamples, .95) === null ? null : Number(percentile(frameIntervalSamples, .95).toFixed(2)),
      fpsAverage: frameMean ? Number((1000 / frameMean).toFixed(1)) : null,
      frameIntervalAverageMs: frameMean === null ? null : Number(frameMean.toFixed(2)),
      frameIntervalP95Ms: percentile(frameIntervalSamples, .95) === null ? null : Number(percentile(frameIntervalSamples, .95).toFixed(2)),
      slowFrameSharePercent: slow === null ? null : Number(slow.toFixed(1)),
      canvasRenderAverageMs: mean(canvasRenderSamples) === null ? null : Number(mean(canvasRenderSamples).toFixed(3)),
      canvasRenderP95Ms: percentile(canvasRenderSamples, .95) === null ? null : Number(percentile(canvasRenderSamples, .95).toFixed(3)),
      sampledCanvasRenders: canvasRenderSamples.length,
      terrainCache: { builds: terrainCacheBuilds, reusedDraws: terrainCacheReuseDraws, draws: terrainCacheDraws, reusePercent: terrainCacheDraws ? Number((terrainCacheReuseDraws / terrainCacheDraws * 100).toFixed(1)) : null, resolution: terrainCache ? { width: terrainCache.width, height: terrainCache.height } : null },
      simulationIntervalAverageMs: mean(simulationIntervalSamples) === null ? null : Number(mean(simulationIntervalSamples).toFixed(1)),
      sampledSimulationIntervals: simulationIntervalSamples.length,
      simulationStepAverageMs: mean(simulationStepCostSamples) === null ? null : Number(mean(simulationStepCostSamples).toFixed(3)),
      simulationStepP95Ms: percentile(simulationStepCostSamples, .95) === null ? null : Number(percentile(simulationStepCostSamples, .95).toFixed(3)),
      sampledSimulationSteps: simulationStepCostSamples.length,
      longTasks: { supported: longTaskObserverSupported, count: longTaskObserverSupported ? longTaskSamples.length : null, totalMs: longTaskObserverSupported ? Number(longTaskSamples.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0).toFixed(1)) : null, maxMs: longTaskObserverSupported && longTaskSamples.length ? Number(Math.max(...longTaskSamples).toFixed(1)) : null, p95Ms: longTaskObserverSupported && longTaskSamples.length ? Number(percentile(longTaskSamples, .95).toFixed(1)) : null },
      canvasBackingResolution: { width: canvas.width, height: canvas.height },
      canvasCssResolution: { width: Math.round(canvas.getBoundingClientRect().width), height: Math.round(canvas.getBoundingClientRect().height) },
      viewport: { width: window.innerWidth, height: window.innerHeight },
      devicePixelRatio: Number((window.devicePixelRatio || 1).toFixed(2)),
      simulationSpeed: Number(ui.speedSelect.value) || 1,
      cinematicMotionEnabled,
      graphicsMode,
      graphicsProfile: GRAPHICS_PROFILES[graphicsMode] || GRAPHICS_PROFILES.cinematic,
      effectiveGraphicsDprCap: Number(effectiveGraphicsCap().toFixed(2)),
      adaptiveQuality: { active: graphicsMode === 'auto', cap: Number(autoQualityCap.toFixed(2)), decisions: autoQualityDecisionCount, stableWindows: autoQualityStableWindows, reason: autoQualityReason },
      reducedMotionPreference: Boolean(reduceMotionQuery && reduceMotionQuery.matches),
      webglScene: Scene3D ? Scene3D.getDiagnostics() : { supported: false, enabled: false },
      sampledAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  function renderDiagnostics() {
    if (!ui.diagFps) return;
    const snapshot = diagnosticsSnapshot();
    const fmt = (value, suffix = '') => value === null || value === undefined ? `—${suffix}` : `${value}${suffix}`;
    ui.diagFps.textContent = fmt(snapshot.fpsAverage, ' FPS');
    ui.diagFpsSummary.textContent = fmt(snapshot.fpsAverage, ' FPS');
    ui.diagFrameMs.textContent = fmt(snapshot.frameIntervalAverageMs, ' мс');
    ui.diagRenderMs.textContent = fmt(snapshot.canvasRenderAverageMs, ' мс');
    ui.diagRenderSummary.textContent = fmt(snapshot.canvasRenderAverageMs, ' мс рендер');
    ui.diagSlowFrames.textContent = fmt(snapshot.slowFrameSharePercent, '%');
    ui.diagCanvasSize.textContent = `${canvas.width} × ${canvas.height}`;
    ui.diagDisplay.textContent = `${window.innerWidth} × ${window.innerHeight}`;
    ui.diagDpr.textContent = `DPR ${snapshot.devicePixelRatio.toFixed(2)} · буфер ×${snapshot.devicePixelRatio.toFixed(2)}`;
    ui.diagTickMs.textContent = fmt(snapshot.simulationIntervalAverageMs, ' мс');
    ui.diagTickDetail.textContent = snapshot.sampledSimulationIntervals ? `${snapshot.sampledSimulationIntervals} замеров · интервалы включают ручные паузы` : 'ожидает запуска симуляции';
    ui.diagStepCost.textContent = fmt(snapshot.simulationStepAverageMs, ' мс');
    if (ui.diagFrameP95) ui.diagFrameP95.textContent = fmt(snapshot.frameIntervalP95Ms, ' мс');
    if (ui.diagCacheReuse) ui.diagCacheReuse.textContent = fmt(snapshot.terrainCache.reusePercent, '%');
    if (ui.diagCacheSummary) ui.diagCacheSummary.textContent = `${snapshot.terrainCache.builds} построений · ${snapshot.terrainCache.reusedDraws} повторных кадров`;
    if (ui.diagLongTaskCount) ui.diagLongTaskCount.textContent = snapshot.longTasks.supported ? `${snapshot.longTasks.count} шт.` : 'Н/Д';
    if (ui.diagLongTaskMax) ui.diagLongTaskMax.textContent = snapshot.longTasks.supported ? (snapshot.longTasks.maxMs === null ? '0 мс' : `${snapshot.longTasks.maxMs} мс`) : 'Н/Д';
    if (ui.diagLongTaskDetail) ui.diagLongTaskDetail.textContent = snapshot.longTasks.supported ? `сумма ${snapshot.longTasks.totalMs} мс · P95 ${snapshot.longTasks.p95Ms ?? 0} мс` : 'этот браузер не поддерживает Long Tasks API';
    if (ui.diagLongTaskTotal) ui.diagLongTaskTotal.textContent = snapshot.longTasks.supported ? 'за текущую сессию наблюдения' : 'измерения основного потока не поддерживаются';
    const webgl = snapshot.webglScene || {};
    if (ui.diag3dFps) ui.diag3dFps.textContent = webgl.enabled && webgl.fps !== null ? `${webgl.fps} FPS` : (webgl.supported ? 'ожидание' : 'Н/Д');
    if (ui.diag3dRenderMs) ui.diag3dRenderMs.textContent = webgl.enabled ? `${webgl.renderLastMs ?? '—'} мс` : '—';
    if (ui.diag3dVertices) ui.diag3dVertices.textContent = webgl.enabled ? String(webgl.vertices || 0) : '—';
    if (ui.diag3dStatus) { const cacheLabel = webgl.renderer === 'canvas2d-software-3d' && webgl.staticCache?.reusePercent !== null ? ` · кэш ${webgl.staticCache.reusePercent}%` : ''; ui.diag3dStatus.textContent = !webgl.supported ? '3D недоступна · используй 2D' : webgl.enabled ? `${webgl.renderer === 'webgl' ? 'WebGL' : 'программная 3D'} · ${webgl.drawCalls} проход · ${webgl.canvas?.width || 0} × ${webgl.canvas?.height || 0}${cacheLabel}` : 'Переключись на 3D-сцену для замеров'; }
    if (ui.diagHealth) {
      let health = 'collecting';
      let healthText = 'НАБОР ЗАМЕРОВ';
      if (snapshot.sampledFrameIntervals >= 20 && snapshot.fpsAverage !== null) {
        if (snapshot.fpsAverage < 28 || (snapshot.slowFrameSharePercent || 0) >= 35) { health = 'slow'; healthText = 'ЕСТЬ ПРОСАДКИ'; }
        else if (snapshot.fpsAverage < 50 || (snapshot.slowFrameSharePercent || 0) >= 12) { health = 'watch'; healthText = 'ТРЕБУЕТ ВНИМАНИЯ'; }
        else { health = 'stable'; healthText = 'СТАБИЛЬНО'; }
      }
      ui.diagHealth.dataset.health = health;
      ui.diagHealth.textContent = healthText;
      ui.diagHealth.title = `Оценка по ${snapshot.sampledFrameIntervals} измеренным интервалам кадра в этой вкладке`;
    }
    if (ui.qualitySelect) ui.qualitySelect.value = graphicsMode;
    if (ui.qualityHint) {
      const label = (GRAPHICS_PROFILES[graphicsMode] || GRAPHICS_PROFILES.cinematic).label;
      ui.qualityHint.textContent = graphicsMode === 'auto' ? `${label}. Текущий предел: ×${autoQualityCap.toFixed(2)}. ${autoQualityReason}.` : label;
      ui.qualityHint.title = graphicsMode === 'auto' ? 'Авто оценивает интервалы кадров примерно раз в 1,8 секунды; качество меняется постепенно и не отправляет данные.' : label;
    }
    document.body.dataset.graphics = graphicsMode;
    ui.motionToggle.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(cinematicMotionEnabled));
    ui.motionToggle.textContent = `Кинематографичные эффекты: ${cinematicMotionEnabled ? 'включены' : 'отключены'}`;
    document.body.dataset.motion = cinematicMotionEnabled ? 'on' : 'off';
    ui.speedHint.textContent = `Один такт — около ${Math.round(BASE_TICK_MS / (Number(ui.speedSelect.value) || 1))} мс`;
  }

  function startFrameMonitor() {
    if (animationFrameHandle !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameHandle);
    previousFrameTimestamp = 0;
    const frame = (timestamp) => {
      animationFrameHandle = null;
      if (document.hidden) return;
      if (previousFrameTimestamp) pushSample(frameIntervalSamples, timestamp - previousFrameTimestamp);
      previousFrameTimestamp = timestamp;
      if (cinematicMotionEnabled && (motionFrame || actionPulse)) { if (boardView === '3d') syncScene3D(); else drawBoard(); }
      const qualityChanged = updateAdaptiveQuality(timestamp);
      if (qualityChanged || timestamp - diagnosticsRefreshTimestamp >= 450) {
        diagnosticsRefreshTimestamp = timestamp;
        renderDiagnostics();
      }
      animationFrameHandle = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
    animationFrameHandle = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  function startSimulationTimer() {
    stopTimer();
    const rate = Number(ui.speedSelect.value) || 1;
    timer = setInterval(stepOnce, BASE_TICK_MS / rate);
  }

  function plansFromCurrent() { return Game.clonePlans(planState); }
  function beginSimulation(autoplay) {
    stopReplayPlayback();
    stopTimer();
    motionFrame = null;
    actionPulse = null;
    const plans = plansFromCurrent();
    Playtest.beginAttempt(sessionMetrics, {
      plannedCommands: plans.mira.length + plans.kite.length,
      miraCommands: plans.mira.length,
      kiteCommands: plans.kite.length
    });
    simState = Game.cloneStateForSimulation(plans, { seed: planState.seed, mission: planState.mission });
    replayHistory = [cloneSnapshot(simState)];
    replayCursor = 0;
    lastSimulationStepTimestamp = 0;
    isPaused = !autoplay;
    setPhase('running');
    renderAll();
    if (autoplay) {
      stepOnce();
      if (phase === 'running') startSimulationTimer();
    } else {
      stepOnce();
    }
    showToast('Операция запущена. Все действия исполняются по тактам.');
  }

  function stepOnce() {
    if (!simState || phase !== 'running') return;
    const now = performance.now();
    if (lastSimulationStepTimestamp) pushSample(simulationIntervalSamples, now - lastSimulationStepTimestamp, 80);
    lastSimulationStepTimestamp = now;
    const before = { ...snapshotPositions(simState), cameraActive: simState.camera.active, dataSecured: simState.objective.secured, carrierId: simState.objective.carrierId };
    const simulationStartedAt = performance.now();
    Game.stepSimulation(simState);
    recordReplaySnapshot();
    pushSample(simulationStepCostSamples, performance.now() - simulationStartedAt, 80);
    const after = { ...snapshotPositions(simState), cameraActive: simState.camera.active, dataSecured: simState.objective.secured, carrierId: simState.objective.carrierId };
    if (before.cameraActive && !after.cameraActive) playSound('hack');
    if (!before.dataSecured && after.dataSecured) playSound('secure');
    setMotionBetween(before, after);
    renderAll();
    if (simState.status === 'won' || simState.status === 'failed') {
      stopTimer();
      Playtest.finishAttempt(sessionMetrics, simState.status, simState.tick);
      setPhase('finished');
      renderAll();
      if (simState.status === 'won') { playSound('success'); showToast('Успех: данные доставлены к точке эвакуации.'); }
      else { playSound('error'); showToast(simState.result.reason, true); }
    }
  }

  function startAutoPlay() {
    if (phase === 'planning' || phase === 'finished') {
      beginSimulation(true);
      return;
    }
    if (phase === 'running' && isPaused) {
      isPaused = false;
      setPhase('running');
      stepOnce();
      if (phase === 'running') startSimulationTimer();
    }
  }

  function togglePause() {
    if (phase !== 'running') return;
    if (isPaused) {
      startAutoPlay();
    } else {
      stopTimer();
      isPaused = true;
      setPhase('running');
      renderAll();
    }
  }

  function backToPlanning() {
    stopReplayPlayback();
    stopTimer();
    motionFrame = null;
    actionPulse = null;
    if (simState) {
      if (phase === 'running' && simState.status === 'running') {
        Playtest.finishAttempt(sessionMetrics, 'abandoned', simState.tick);
      }
      // Preserve the authored plan, not the simulated positions or state.
      planState.status = 'planning';
    }
    simState = null;
    isPaused = true;
    setPhase('planning');
    renderAll();
    showToast('Режим планирования. Очереди сохранены — можно внести изменения.');
  }

  function setSelectedAgent(agentId) {
    if (!Game.getAgent(planState, agentId)) return;
    if (agentId !== selectedAgentId) Playtest.recordInteraction(sessionMetrics, 'operativeSelections', 1, false);
    selectedAgentId = agentId;
    keyboardCursor = { ...Game.getPlannedPosition(planState, agentId) };
    renderAll();
  }

  function addAction(type) {
    if (!activeCommandsAllowed()) return;
    const result = Game.enqueueAction(planState, selectedAgentId, type);
    if (!result.ok) {
      Playtest.recordInteraction(sessionMetrics, 'commandsRejected', 1, false);
      showToast(result.reason, true);
      return;
    }
    Playtest.recordInteraction(sessionMetrics, 'actionCommandsAdded');
    playSound(type === Game.ACTION.HACK ? 'hack' : type === Game.ACTION.SECURE ? 'secure' : 'tap');
    showToast(result.reason);
    renderAll();
  }

  function drawRoundedRect(context, x, y, width, height, radius, fill, stroke) {
    // Manual path keeps the renderer compatible with browsers that predate roundRect().
    const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(radius, width / 2, height / 2));
    context.beginPath();
    context.moveTo(x + r, y);
    context.lineTo(x + width - r, y);
    context.quadraticCurveTo(x + width, y, x + width, y + r);
    context.lineTo(x + width, y + height - r);
    context.quadraticCurveTo(x + width, y + height, x + width - r, y + height);
    context.lineTo(x + r, y + height);
    context.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + height, x, y + height - r);
    context.lineTo(x, y + r);
    context.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y);
    context.closePath();
    if (fill) { context.fillStyle = fill; context.fill(); }
    if (stroke) { context.strokeStyle = stroke; context.stroke(); }
  }

  function resizeCanvas() {
    if (!ctx) {
      ui.hint.textContent = 'Не удалось создать Canvas-контекст. Открой игру в актуальном браузере с поддержкой Canvas 2D.';
      ui.status.textContent = 'Графический режим недоступен в этом браузере.';
      ui.run.disabled = true;
      ui.sample.disabled = true;
      return;
    }
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cssWidth = Math.max(240, Math.round(rect.width || canvas.parentElement.clientWidth));
    const cssHeight = cssWidth * Game.ROWS / Game.COLS;
    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, effectiveGraphicsCap());
    canvas.style.height = `${cssHeight}px`;
    canvas.width = Math.round(cssWidth * dpr);
    canvas.height = Math.round(cssHeight * dpr);
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    boardCssWidth = cssWidth;
    boardCell = cssWidth / Game.COLS;
    drawBoard();
  }

  function buildTerrainCache(cell, width, height) {
    const backingScale = canvas.width / Math.max(1, width);
    const layer = document.createElement('canvas');
    layer.width = canvas.width;
    layer.height = canvas.height;
    const terrain = layer.getContext('2d');
    if (!terrain) return false;
    terrain.setTransform(backingScale, 0, 0, backingScale, 0, 0);

    const backdrop = terrain.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, height);
    backdrop.addColorStop(0, '#0b1a25');
    backdrop.addColorStop(.48, '#08141e');
    backdrop.addColorStop(1, '#061019');
    terrain.fillStyle = backdrop;
    terrain.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
    const light = terrain.createRadialGradient(width * .2, height * .12, 0, width * .2, height * .12, width * .72);
    light.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(54,160,169,.105)');
    light.addColorStop(.55, 'rgba(26,79,96,.035)');
    light.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    terrain.fillStyle = light;
    terrain.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

    const initial = Game.createInitialState();
    for (let y = 0; y < Game.ROWS; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < Game.COLS; x++) {
        const px = x * cell, py = y * cell;
        const isCameraCell = x === initial.camera.x && y === initial.camera.y;
        const isObjectiveCell = x === initial.objective.x && y === initial.objective.y;
        const wall = !Game.isWalkable(initial, x, y) && !isCameraCell && !(isObjectiveCell && !initial.objective.secured);
        const border = x === 0 || y === 0 || x === Game.COLS - 1 || y === Game.ROWS - 1;
        const floor = terrain.createLinearGradient(px, py, px + cell, py + cell);
        floor.addColorStop(0, (x + y) % 2 ? '#10202b' : '#0d1c27');
        floor.addColorStop(1, (x + y) % 2 ? '#0c1923' : '#0a1721');
        terrain.fillStyle = floor;
        terrain.fillRect(px, py, cell, cell);
        if (wall || border) {
          const metal = terrain.createLinearGradient(px, py, px + cell, py + cell);
          metal.addColorStop(0, '#304653');
          metal.addColorStop(.42, '#1d303d');
          metal.addColorStop(1, '#152631');
          terrain.fillStyle = metal;
          terrain.fillRect(px + 1, py + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2);
          terrain.strokeStyle = 'rgba(180,211,229,.22)'; terrain.lineWidth = 1;
          terrain.beginPath(); terrain.moveTo(px + 3, py + cell - 3); terrain.lineTo(px + 3, py + 3); terrain.lineTo(px + cell - 3, py + 3); terrain.stroke();
          terrain.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.34)';
          terrain.beginPath(); terrain.moveTo(px + cell - 3, py + 3); terrain.lineTo(px + cell - 3, py + cell - 3); terrain.lineTo(px + 3, py + cell - 3); terrain.stroke();
          terrain.strokeStyle = 'rgba(119,157,181,.14)'; terrain.strokeRect(px + 7, py + 7, cell - 14, cell - 14);
          terrain.fillStyle = 'rgba(186,210,225,.25)';
          const rivet = Math.max(1, cell * .018);
          terrain.beginPath(); terrain.arc(px + 7, py + 7, rivet, 0, Math.PI * 2); terrain.fill();
          terrain.beginPath(); terrain.arc(px + cell - 7, py + cell - 7, rivet, 0, Math.PI * 2); terrain.fill();
        } else {
          terrain.strokeStyle = 'rgba(117,155,177,.13)'; terrain.lineWidth = 1;
          terrain.strokeRect(px + .5, py + .5, cell - 1, cell - 1);
          if ((x * 7 + y * 3) % 13 === 0) {
            terrain.fillStyle = 'rgba(125,170,189,.24)';
            terrain.fillRect(px + cell * .2, py + cell * .2, Math.max(1, cell * .035), Math.max(1, cell * .035));
          }
          if (y === 1 && x > 1 && x < Game.COLS - 2 && x % 3 === 0) {
            terrain.fillStyle = 'rgba(118,240,231,.12)';
            terrain.fillRect(px + cell * .2, py + cell * .085, cell * .6, Math.max(1, cell * .025));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    terrainCache = layer;
    terrainCacheCell = cell;
    terrainCacheBuilds += 1;
    return true;
  }

  function drawCameraZoneOverlay(state, cell) {
    if (!showCameraLayer || !state.camera.active) return;
    const zone = Game.MISSION.camera.zone;
    for (let y = zone.yMin; y <= zone.yMax; y++) {
      for (let x = zone.xMin; x <= zone.xMax; x++) {
        const isBorder = x === 0 || y === 0 || x === Game.COLS - 1 || y === Game.ROWS - 1;
        const isWall = Game.MISSION.walls.some((wall) => wall.x === x && wall.y === y);
        if (isBorder || isWall) continue;
        const px = x * cell, py = y * cell;
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239,189,114,.13)';
        ctx.fillRect(px + 1, py + 1, cell - 2, cell - 2);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239,189,114,.16)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.strokeRect(px + 4, py + 4, cell - 8, cell - 8);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239,189,114,.07)';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px + cell * .16, py + cell * .82); ctx.lineTo(px + cell * .82, py + cell * .16); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
  }

  function drawBoard() {
    if (!ctx || !boardCell) return;
    const renderStartedAt = performance.now();
    const state = visualState();
    const cell = boardCell;
    const width = cell * Game.COLS;
    const height = cell * Game.ROWS;
    terrainCacheDraws += 1;
    if (!terrainCache || terrainCache.width !== canvas.width || terrainCache.height !== canvas.height || terrainCacheCell !== cell) {
      if (!buildTerrainCache(cell, width, height)) {
        ctx.fillStyle = '#08131b'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
      }
    } else {
      terrainCacheReuseDraws += 1;
    }
    if (terrainCache) {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
      ctx.drawImage(terrainCache, 0, 0, width, height);
    }
    drawCameraZoneOverlay(state, cell);

    // Cinematic local light pools add depth without obscuring tactical cells.
    // The lights follow live world positions, so a moving guard changes the scene.
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
    const emitters = [
      { x: state.exit.x, y: state.exit.y, color: 'rgba(143,232,189,.17)', radius: cell * 1.65 },
      { x: state.guard.x, y: state.guard.y, color: 'rgba(255,91,114,.13)', radius: cell * 1.9 },
      { x: state.camera.x, y: state.camera.y, color: state.camera.active ? 'rgba(239,189,114,.18)' : 'rgba(118,240,231,.11)', radius: cell * 1.75 },
      { x: state.objective.x, y: state.objective.y, color: state.objective.secured ? 'rgba(239,189,114,.08)' : 'rgba(118,240,231,.17)', radius: cell * 1.8 }
    ];
    for (const emitter of emitters) {
      const glowX = (emitter.x + .5) * cell;
      const glowY = (emitter.y + .5) * cell;
      const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(glowX, glowY, 0, glowX, glowY, emitter.radius);
      glow.addColorStop(0, emitter.color);
      glow.addColorStop(.36, emitter.color.replace(/\.\d+\)/, '.055)'));
      glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = glow;
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
    }
    ctx.restore();

    // The surveillance field has an explicit perimeter to make its limits readable.
    if (showCameraLayer && state.camera.active) {
      const zone = Game.MISSION.camera.zone;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239,189,114,.42)';
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, cell * .018);
      ctx.setLineDash([Math.max(3, cell * .09), Math.max(2, cell * .065)]);
      ctx.strokeRect(zone.xMin * cell + 2, zone.yMin * cell + 2, (zone.xMax - zone.xMin + 1) * cell - 4, (zone.yMax - zone.yMin + 1) * cell - 4);
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239,189,114,.8)';
      ctx.font = `600 ${Math.max(6, cell * .105)}px ${CANVAS_FONT}`;
      ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
      ctx.fillText('WATCH ZONE', zone.xMin * cell + cell * .12, (zone.yMax + 1) * cell - cell * .06);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    // Extraction marker: restrained concentric geometry, readable even on mobile.
    const exit = state.exit;
    const ex = (exit.x + .5) * cell, ey = (exit.y + .5) * cell;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(143,232,189,.2)'; ctx.shadowBlur = graphicsGlow(cell * .2);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(143,232,189,.11)';
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(143,232,189,.78)'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, cell * .025);
    drawRoundedRect(ctx, exit.x * cell + 5, exit.y * cell + 5, cell - 10, cell - 10, cell * .12, 'rgba(143,232,189,.09)', 'rgba(143,232,189,.75)');
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex, ey - cell * .04, cell * .16, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - cell * .17, ey + cell * .1); ctx.lineTo(ex + cell * .17, ey + cell * .1); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.green;
    ctx.font = `700 ${Math.max(6, cell * .12)}px ${CANVAS_FONT}`;
    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
    ctx.fillText('EXFIL', ex, ey + cell * .38);
    ctx.restore();

    // Optional patrol layer: useful for analysis, hidden by default to keep the board calm.
    if (showPatrolLayer) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,127,142,.34)';
      ctx.setLineDash([Math.max(3, cell * .07), Math.max(3, cell * .11)]);
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, cell * .022);
      ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,91,114,.22)'; ctx.shadowBlur = graphicsGlow(cell * .16);
      const patrol = [{ x: Game.MISSION.guardStart.x, y: Game.MISSION.guardStart.y }, ...Game.MISSION.guardRoute];
      ctx.beginPath();
      patrol.forEach((point, index) => {
        const px = (point.x + .5) * cell, py = (point.y + .5) * cell;
        if (index === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
      });
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < patrol.length - 1; i += 2) {
        const p = patrol[i]; const px = (p.x + .5) * cell, py = (p.y + .5) * cell;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, Math.max(1.5, cell * .045), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,127,142,.8)'; ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    drawCamera(state, cell);
    drawObjective(state, cell);
    drawPlans(state, cell);
    drawHoverPath(state, cell);
    drawGuard(state.guard, cell);
    if (Array.isArray(state.guards)) state.guards.slice(1).forEach((guardUnit) => drawGuard(guardUnit, cell));
    for (const agent of state.agents) drawAgent(agent, cell, agent.id === selectedAgentId && phase === 'planning', state.objective.carrierId === agent.id);

    // Tactical sonar markers are tied to the queued command or live guard-investigation state.
    const decoyCommands = planState.agents.flatMap((operative) => operative.plan.filter((command) => command.type === Game.ACTION.DECOY));
    const decoyTarget = decoyTargetMode ? (keyboardNavigationActive ? keyboardCursor : hoverCell) : decoyCommands.at(-1);
    const drawNoiseMarker = (target, alpha = .92) => {
      if (!target) return;
